Not up: today's loop.

(3) THE HOLD: coop_play_hold's own branch, FIRST (before the scene rule 12850 whose `theirs = coop_use_theirs() || g_coop_screen` is false on this route, and before the p1_look rule 12881 which the TICK's call (g_coop_turn false, identity swapped to p2 by coop_be_player2: r13-21492 == g_coop_p2 so its last test fails, but r13-21496 == p2's id too; both tests fail -> no g_coop_p1_look; in the LOOK g_coop_turn is true -> skipped; either way the native run would put bit 2 into the game's word and fire 0x801556CC's event 102) [read 12846-12900]:
`if (coop_own_closeup() && coop_p2_words_call() && cpu->lr >= CLOSE_UP_STEP_FROM && cpu->lr < CLOSE_UP_STEP_TO && cpu->gpr[3] <= 1u && sda_ready(cpu)) { save kCoopHoldWords[4] (23344, 25696, 23040, 24048) [read 17542]; if (r3 == 1) mem_write32(-23344, 0) (as coop_words_hold's close 17262: 0x801556CC(2, 0) on a nonzero word broadcasts 102 from the identity in); lr = cpu->lr; hle_guest_call(COOP_PLAY_HOLD, r3); cpu->lr = lr; put the 4 words back; g_coop_p2_look.held = r3 == 0; log "[coop] player 2's own close-up %s in their copy (lr 0x%08X, game's word kept %u)"; g_hle_passthrough = false; g_hle_tail_call = false; return; }`
- Physical effect [read asm 415409-415452 via the dossier's F10/F11/F12]: r3 == 0 writes +16 = 0x0FFFFFFF into r13-23060/-23052/-23056's channels of THE COPY IN (theirs: g_coop_state[1] in the look 18587 and the tick 14468) and zeroes the control block's word; r3 == 1 writes 0 into -23060/-23052's +16, -23056 stays 0x0FFFFFFF, as the game leaves player 1 after RETURN. 0x8013E160 returns at once (r31 = 4) while +16 != 0; the controller 0x80045D30 steps nothing. COOP_PAD_UPDATE decrements +16 > 0 once a frame: permanent in practice. The nested guest call re-enters coop_play_hold with lr 0x0BADF010: outside both ranges and != COOP_ENCHANT_HOLDS -> passthrough (12898-12900), the shape the scene rule 12866 and coop_words_hold 17262 rely on today.

## Risks ranked

- 1 (highest): the hide-others host walk (B9). Per node per open it makes up to five guest calls (0x801F662C, 0x80117C64, 0x801F0638, 0x801F077C, 0x8014ED1C); a mismatch with the game's loop (a missed skip, the type-5 radius, the double arg) changes the count (cut vs blend, given 6) or hides the wrong character from player 2's half. Mitigation: for owner 1 the intercept passes through (today's path); for owner 2 log each hit and the count, and in the first run compare against ED_SHOW_TRACE of player 1's close-up at the same hole (same camera hash -> the same nodes should be hit).
- 2: the slot rule while their close-up is up (B1) assumes the only valid presses are the stepper's asks (34/7 with script -2); a zone's ask with a script > 0 that the game would let through under a close-up (not seen in the code read: the chooser skips 1/3/5 under the hold) would be unpressable for player 2 until RETURN. Log every slot passed over while .up.
- 3: the camera in modes 0/1 (B8, the FINDING 1 fix): the own-view branch's wall clamp and dream shift now run for a close-up camera; after RETURN their half snaps to the third-person camera instead of easing back to record 2; the first frame after the open is drawn from the step inside the same turn [inf]. Check the shots of each half at the open and the RETURN in mode 0 (the default) and mode 2.
- 4: coop_camera_p2_in's rebuild guard and the by-hash exemption (B8): if g_coop_p2_stepper_in misses a path (a by-hash notify from inside the stepper that is not a test) p2_bare is cleared and their set stops following the room's start for the rest of the room; if the `rest` guard is wrong a room-start choice drops their record 10 mid-close-up (no hang: the pop sees depth 0). Log both.
- 5: the deferred RETURN script's use (B4/B5): it waits at the controller behind g_coop_find_free / g_coop_use.active, so their EXAMINE comes back late while player 1 is in a free find; its lend swaps the live view for the script's duration (one frame for ed219; a RETURN script that waits is today's route, with the lend on). busy_before and the assist skip are new and unrun [inf].
- 6: event 102 from 0x801556CC(2, 1) at the open and press 2 (the hold's broadcast from player 2's identity) is the same event step A's words open fires today; what player 1's node does with a hold event from player 2 is not read.
- 7: the tick's extra swaps while .up (B2): coop_cameras_in(2)/(1) and coop_be_player2 every frame their words are up inside a close-up; coop_breath's 8 CoopPlayerState slots (reuse g_coop_events_p1, add none).
- 8: two show flags at once (B10): a script's fn111(1) show clears both owners' kept hides (today's one flag did the same), so the other player's close-up hide ends early while the game's show also shows 'the player'. Rare; log it.
- 9: the cancel (B12) is wired only at a room change; a cutscene or a state load while their close-up is up leaves their hold in their copy and their set at depth 1 until the room changes (step E). The frontend's save inside the look's press is refused by nothing new.
- 10: the element pool (10 shared elements): two words windows, two prompts and fading ones; 0x801DB374 with none free prints and the window has no text. Count elements in the first runs.
- 11: kind 3 (PICK UP) beside their own kind-2 close-up: a thing lying in reach (room 183's block) is not pressable under their close-up (the slot rule) and its PICK UP after RETURN takes today's lend route; a PICK UP of theirs while player 1 is in an own close-up is today's behaviour. Step C.
- 12: mode 2's culling to player 1's camera (known): a close-up far from player 1 may show fewer objects in player 2's half.

## The scripted check

All runs from the repo root with every log: `sh tools/ed/run.sh SAVE NAME` (logs/run-*-NAME/, both.tsv, shots every 250 ms; `ed timeline`, EVENTS.log and page-*.log first; freezes in saves/reports/hang-*.txt). Add ED_COOP_TOME_COPY=1 for the guest-call 'stopped' line (the ED_GUEST_CALL_LOG line shows only the first 40 calls). docs/coop-next-session.md says only saves/slot1.state (2026-11-09, the room 180 door) is current: first run `ED_RUN_FRAMES=6300` on saves/slot2.state and confirm from ROOM_LOG that it loads in room 183 at a place outside CameraRgn03/05 and before SKPlane01 (y < 2300: the block-holes scene; failed-attempts 666-675); if not, make a state in room 183 from slot1 by the door and use it. Coordinates from `ed room 183` [read]: CameraRgn03 sphere at (-114, 2004, 175) r 312 enter 90 (ed219 Camera04, hash 564924824, at (104,1571,293) looking at (-75,2002,535)); CameraRgn05 sphere at (1433, 1661, 175) r 312 enter 92 (ed219 Camera06, hash 565047707, at (986,1632,285) -> (1411,1630,514)); CameraRgn07 box x -1375..2120, y -1235..1975 (enter 187: Camera01/02): CameraRgn05's centre is inside it, CameraRgn03's is not. BlockRegion01 (the PICK UP block, kind 3) box x 215..504, y 195..485: both puts are far from it. Pads: ED_COOP_P2_PUT=frame,x,y,z (once, direct), ED_PUT=when,who,x,y,z;... (who 2 = player 2, else player 1; placed on the floor by collision), ED_PAD2_SCRIPT=start,end,id;... (ids a/b/x/y/z/start/l/r/up/down/left/right/lup/ldown/lleft/lright/rup/rdown/rleft/rright), ED_INPUT_SCRIPT=start,end,buttons[,x[,y[,analog]]] for player 1 (B = 0x0200; a stick-only event is kept by its stick).

WATCH, NOT SCRIPTED (REVIEW 1): the refusal-branch ease (B6) cannot be scripted (a refused player 2 presses nothing; player 1's bare scene does not refuse the turn); log once when it runs and watch for it in the user's play. The user's play (cannot be scripted): the feel of the hold and the RETURN ease in each camera mode, a Tome open during P2UP (step D), real controllers' held-over logic. After each run add seen items to docs/items-log.md and any failed attempt with its code to docs/coop-failed-attempts.md; `ed test` after every change.

## The final reviews (what they refuted, what they confirmed)

### Review, regressions

- REFUTED (regression, switch on; the design's own B11 / section (3) line for coop_scene_set): adding `|| (coop_own_closeup() && g_coop_p2_look.held)` to p2_close_up at hle.c:15329 freezes PLAYER 1 for good when their own scene ends while player 2's own kind-2 close-up is held. Trace [read hle.c 15315-15380]: fn22(0) from player 1's scene script reaches coop_scene_set with on == 0; owner = 0 (p2_acts false: identity player 1 in the main loop; g_coop_turn false; g_coop_screen false on the own route) at 15322; other_on = p2_on || p2_close_up at 15330 -> TRUE by the new .held term (p2_on false, nothing of theirs in the game's word); the kept-end path 15335-15380 runs the game's end by guest call, then WRITES BACK the three control channels' +16 counts (15344-15347, 15361-15363: these are the GAME's input = player 1's, masked 0x0FFFFFFF by the scene's hold), and busy := words[0] & ~32 (15359) = 0 (only the scene's bit was there), g_coop_p1_look = false (15371). Result: player 1's -23060/-23052/-23056 channels stay at 0x0FFFFFFF with the busy word 0 and no flag set. Nobody lets go: coop_p2_holds (17415-17426) needs g_coop_script_hold or player 2's scene and the word == 2 -> false; coop_p1_close_up (17097) needs g_coop_p1_look -> false, so hle_coop_controller's coop_hold_open (18055-18059) is not called; coop_player1_turn runs only under g_coop_screen (18021) which the own route never sets; player 2's RETURN let-go (0x80199EA0(1) at 0x80025118) runs in THEIR copy by the design's own branch with the game's word kept, so it never reaches player 1's channels. The controller 0x80045D30 steps nothing while +16 != 0 (dossier F11/F12) and COOP_PAD_UPDATE's decrement from 0x0FFFFFFF is permanent in practice. Then, after player 2's RETURN (.held false), the turn's mask loop (18604-18616) copies the GAME's 0x0FFFFFFF over player 2's three channels too: both players frozen, busy 0, no hang report. Today (no .held term) the same scenario takes the native end: p2_close_up false (15329: nothing of player 2's is in the word on the own route), other_on false, the scene's end clears the hold natively and player 1 walks; player 2's own close-up is untouched because their hold lives in g_coop_state[1], not in the game's input. Concrete case: switch on, player 1 in room 183's block-holes scene (L01/419, waits 80+55+150 frames) or the walk-in scene, player 2 opens their own ed219 examine at a filled hole (the user's documented play: coop-todo.md 25 'after each, an examine of a filled hole by each'; 'the pickup/examine beside a scene' is a confirmed behaviour), player 1's scene ends before player 2's RETURN. Fix: do NOT add .held (or .up) to coop_scene_set's p2_close_up; on the own route the scene's end touches nothing of player 2's (their channels are in their copy, their bit is host-side), so the native end is right for both. The design's own CONFIRMED BEHAVIOURS paragraph ('coop_scene_set/coop_p2_holds/the refusal read the game's word, which never carries their bits') contradicts B11.
- Design inaccuracy (not a regression): section (3) lists 0x80024BC8 (the cancel's let-go, 0x80024B24) among the call sites the coop_play_hold own branch covers, but CLOSE_UP_STEP_FROM = 0x80024D78 and CLOSE_UP_STEP_TO = 0x800255A4 [read hle.c 12838-12839], so 0x80024BC8 is outside the range: the own branch (lr test) does not fire there, nor does the scene rule (12850: same range) or the p1_look rule (12881: r3 == 0 only); 0x80199EA0(1) runs natively (passthrough 12898-12900) with their copy in and the busy word zeroed by the cancel, so the channel let-go lands in their copy and 0x801556CC(2, 0) on a zero word broadcasts nothing; .held must be cleared by the cancel itself (B12 does). Harmless as written, but the claim is wrong; also coop_zones_new (14945) runs BEFORE coop_prompt_look saves the 2060 block (14957), so anything the cancel's native calls write in r13-24040..-21981 persists past the look (a room change anyway).
- Checked and NOT refuted: (a) frame order for 'one B closes their words and presses RETURN in the same frame': the turn (hle_coop_controller -> coop_player_turn -> coop_prompt_look at ~18690, b_was = b at 15043 every look, including while words.up) runs BEFORE the game's words tick 0x80024AF0 (coop_words_tick consumes g_coop_turn_ran, 14455-14456, set at 18583), so the B that closes their words in the tick is already b_was = true at the next look and presses nothing until released and pressed again. (b) coop_show_asked's hide fill reads node = theirs ? g_coop_p2 : r13-21492 (16321), not r13-21488, so player 2's object is the one hidden in their own look even though the turn's who[] (18660-18668) leaves -21488 as player 1's. (c) The words-cancel close 0x80024384 is `li r3,1; bl 0x80024220` [read asm 32746-32759], so its 0x80042A8C(0) comes with lr WORDS_HOLD_CLOSES and coop_words_hold's own branch (17250-17271) catches it, as the design says. (d) Per-owner show attribution by g_coop_screen (B10) cannot misfire with the switch unset because two close-ups never overlap on today's routes: player 1's B is kept under player 2's screen (coop_p1_press_kept, up = coop_p2_scene_or_hold) and player 2's B is kept under player 1's look (coop_player1_mid_use at 18709); the only overlaps are own-route ones, where coop_p2_words_call() decides first. (e) B6's mask-loop exemption, B3's own branch inside the turn (coop_hold_open 17543-17557 has already zeroed the game's word and channels when player 1 is in their own look; the own branch's save/restore of kCoopHoldWords is consistent with it) and B4's deferral keyed on coop_p2_words_call() all trace through without touching player 1's state.
- Line anchors at HEAD 5330b64 (hle.c unmodified): coop_play_hold 12846-12900 with CLOSE_UP_STEP_FROM/TO 12838-12839; coop_words_tick 14452-14492 (ran/g_coop_turn_ran 14455-14456); coop_zones_new 14492-14507 called at 14945; the 2060-block save 14957; the press 15016-15043; the choice loop 15138-15148; slot emptying 15163-15170; coop_scene_set 15308-15420 (owner 15322, p2_close_up 15329, other_on 15330, kept end 15335-15380, counts put back 15361-15363, busy 15359, g_coop_p1_look = false 15371); coop_show_asked 16281-16347 (theirs 16310-16311, node 16321); coop_p1_close_up 17097-17140; coop_words_hold 17243-17330; coop_p2_holds 17415-17426; coop_player_held 17432-17441; coop_hold_open/shut 17543-17568; hle_coop_controller 18012-18200 (screen -> coop_player1_turn 18021, p1_look 18055, p2_hold 18069, close_script consumer 18138-18148); coop_p2_press_held 18512-18529; coop_player_turn 18531-18800 (refusal 18565-18580, g_coop_turn_ran 18583, mask loop 18604-18616, who[] 18660-18668, B block 18697-18717, kept-B 18719-18745, controller 18759, camera step 18761).

### Review, feasibility

- REFUTED (B9, the hide-others host walk; concrete) [read asm 33935-33945]: the game's loop does NOT call 0x80117C64 on *(node+20). At 0x80025614-0x80025628: `or r3,r24; bl 0x801F62D8` (= lwz r3,20(r3): the node's state word, asm 509887) whose RESULT IS DISCARDED by the next `or r3,r24` at 0x8002561C; `bl 0x801F633C` (= lwz r3,52(r3): obj) -> `or r23,r3`; then `bl 0x80117C64` with r3 = r23 = obj. 0x80117C64 is `lwz r3,580(r3); blr` (asm 282146; hle.c 11002-11008 names it OBJECT_MODEL, 'object +580, the character's file number'). So the skip is `*(obj+580) not in 32..35`, a model/file-number test on the OBJECT. The design's walk (section (5), B9, and the dossier's hide-others (a)) does `guest 0x80117C64(*(node+20))`: *(node+20) is the node's state (COOP_NODE_NOW, a small integer such as COOP_STATE_LOOKS), so the guest call executes lwz from state+580 (an address below 0x80000000): hle_guest_call_within (main.c 86-96) breaks on the exception, returns false with *result unset -> the filter reads garbage/0 -> every node reaching that test is mis-filtered (nodes of models 32..35, which the game skips, get counted and hidden; or with a crash-free garbage value others get skipped). The count (cut vs blend, given 6) and the hidden set then differ from the game's for owner 2, and RUN 1/2's 'same camera hash -> same nodes hit' comparison fails. Fix: `model = mem_read32(cpu, obj + 580u)` (no guest call needed) and skip when 32..35; 0x801F62D8 need not be called at all.
- CONFIRMED [read asm 33906-34060, 282909-282947]: the rest of the walk's shape matches the design: skip node == r13-21492 (0x800255E0); room via 0x801F662C(node) == r13-23276 (0x801F662C = obj->0x80118CAC, asm 510100-510108); kind *(node+56) == 1 (0x801F62C0 = lwz 56); type *(node+8) (0x801F62D0 = lwz 8) not 37/64/69; obj = *(node+52); radius 0x80118850 = `lfs f1,624(r3); blr`, type 5 writes obj+624 := radius + *(r2-32128) via 0x801188D8 (`lfs f0,624; stfs f1,624; fmr f1,f0`) and restores after the first test (0x800256AC-0x800256B4); t = 0x801F0638(obj,hash,blend) || 0x801F077C(obj,hash,blend, f1 = *(r2-32132)); beh = 0x8014ED1C(node), hit iff beh && *(beh+24) (0x8014F898 = lwz 24) == 0x8002790C; mark via 0x80033874/0x800338BC, r25++; (r25 & 0xFFFF) -> r13-25952 := 1; run_now -> 0x8002580C; returns r25. 0x8002576C (0x9421FFE0 first word, asm 34020): all nodes, skip r13-21492, kind 1 && flag bit (rlwinm. 5,5), count -> -25952 := 2, returns count. First words 0x9421FFC0 / 0x9421FFE0 as the design states. Both addresses lie inside the dispatch range (HLE_CODE_BASE 0x80003000 .. HLE_CODE_LIMIT 0x80240000, hle.c 5135-5136; hle_dispatch_entry 5148 covers every aligned address), and the registration pattern at 24786-24793 (first-word check + intercept==NULL && notify==NULL) applies.
- CONFIRMED [read hle.c 5454-5486, main.c 61-115]: an intercept may make guest calls and then return a value: hle_guest_call_within memcpy-restores the whole CPUState span (offsetof srr0) after each call, so gpr/lr are back to the intercept's entry state and only *result = gpr[3] survives; the design's `hle_set_u32(count); g_hle_passthrough = false; g_hle_tail_call = false` then reaches the dispatcher's `if (!g_hle_passthrough) { if (!g_hle_tail_call) hle_return(cpu); return true; }` (5477-5481). The gpr[0..12]/lr save in coop_show_asked (16315-16340) is therefore redundant, not required. The host must set gpr[3] AFTER its last guest call (the design's order is right).
- CONFIRMED [read]: every hle.c line the design names matches HEAD 5330b64 (hle.c unmodified in git): coop_play_hold 12846-12900 (scene rule 12850 `theirs = coop_use_theirs() || g_coop_screen`, p1_look rule 12881 with !g_coop_turn, passthrough 12898-12900); CoopWordsOwn 14363-14372; coop_p2_words_call 14391 = own_words && (g_coop_p2_words_tick || (g_coop_prompt_look && g_coop_turn)); coop_words_own_cancel 14424-14450 with ONE caller coop_zones_new 14496 (runs at 14945, before g_coop_prompt_look = true at 14978, so coop_p2_words_call() is false inside it, as the design says); coop_words_tick 14452-14492 (swaps only g_coop_state[1] + CoopWordsOwn; gate g_coop_p2_words.up); coop_prompt_look: cameras_in(2) 14963 / back 15130, slots in 14968 / saved 15056, looks in 14971-14976 / saved 15049-15053, own set 14991-15000, zones-off 14999 only while words.up, press 15016-15043 (outer gate `own && !words.up && !g_coop_screen && !coop_p2_scene_on() && !door_turns && !left_behind`, first live slot then `break`, inner `id == 6 && script == -2 && PROMPT_LOOKS[index] == 1`), b_was = b 15043, own_began 15074-15087, choice loop 15138-15148 skipping only while own && words.up, slots 0/1/3/5 emptied 15163-15170;
